2026-10-25T08:44:50.5166595Z # Start locale1-x11-sync.service unit to enable Anaconda to control keyboard layouts
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5167586Z # This needs to be started only on X11 based systems
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5168284Z start_locale_sync_unit() {
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5172811Z     if [ -z "${WAYLAND_DISPLAY}" ] && [ -n "${DISPLAY}" ] && [ -e "/usr/lib/systemd/user/locale1-x11-sync.service" ]; then
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5173825Z         systemctl --user start locale1-x11-sync.service
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5174337Z     fi
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5174637Z }
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5174796Z
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5174974Z stop_locale_sync_unit() {
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5175727Z     if [ -z "${WAYLAND_DISPLAY}" ] && [ -n "${DISPLAY}" ] && [ -e "/usr/lib/systemd/user/locale1-x11-sync.service" ]; then
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5176622Z         systemctl --user stop locale1-x11-sync.service
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5177115Z     fi
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5177409Z }
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5177566Z
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5177784Z # Detect and save the wayland socket before re-exec
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5178322Z if [ -n "$WAYLAND_DISPLAY" ]; then
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5178789Z     if [ -e "$WAYLAND_DISPLAY_SOCKET" ]; then
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5179286Z         rm -f "${WAYLAND_DISPLAY_SOCKET}"
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5179721Z     fi
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5180050Z     touch "${WAYLAND_DISPLAY_SOCKET}"
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5180669Z     echo "${XDG_RUNTIME_DIR}/${WAYLAND_DISPLAY}" > ${WAYLAND_DISPLAY_SOCKET}
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5181278Z fi
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5181438Z
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5181652Z # Detect and save the a11y bus path before re-exec
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5182525Z if [ -n "$WAYLAND_DISPLAY" ] && [ -z "$AT_SPI_BUS_ADDRESS" ]; then
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5183141Z     if [ -e "$ATSPI_BUSADDR" ]; then
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5183610Z         rm -f "${ATSPI_BUSADDR}"
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5184009Z     fi
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5184918Z     _BUSCTL_OUTPUTREGEX='"([^"]+)"'
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5185660Z     _ATSPIADDR="$( busctl call --user org.a11y.Bus /org/a11y/bus org.a11y.Bus GetAddress )"
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5186591Z     # Check that the busctl call did not fail and grab expected output
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5187244Z     # shellcheck disable=SC2181
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5187791Z     if [ "$?" -eq 0 ] && [[ $_ATSPIADDR =~ $_BUSCTL_OUTPUTREGEX ]]; then
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5188390Z         touch "${ATSPI_BUSADDR}"
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5188852Z         echo "${BASH_REMATCH[1]}" > ${ATSPI_BUSADDR}
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5189346Z     fi
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5189657Z fi
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5189829Z
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5189834Z
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5190360Z # The command needs to be run with root privileges, so if it was started
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5191083Z # unprivileged, restart running as root.

2026-10-25T08:44:50.5192276Z     if [ -z "$WAYLAND_DISPLAY" ]; then

2026-10-25T08:44:50.5193151Z     fi
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5193325Z
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5193471Z     start_locale_sync_unit
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5193888Z     pkexec "$0" "$@"
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5194234Z     ret=$?
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5194577Z     stop_locale_sync_unit
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5194834Z
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5194968Z     exit $ret
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5195256Z fi
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5195360Z
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5195480Z # pkexec clears the environment, so get it back
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5195778Z if [ -n "${PKEXEC_UID}" ]; then
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5196046Z     INSTALLER_USER=$(id -n -u "${PKEXEC_UID}")
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5196709Z     readarray -t user_environment < <(pkexec --user "${INSTALLER_USER}" env XDG_RUNTIME_DIR="/run/user/${PKEXEC_UID}" systemctl --user show-environment)
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5197429Z
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5197554Z     for variable in "${user_environment[@]}"; do
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5197844Z       export "${variable?}"
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5198062Z     done
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5198233Z fi
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5198335Z
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5198459Z # pkexec clears WAYLAND_DISPLAY from environment
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5198840Z if [ -z "$WAYLAND_DISPLAY" ] && [ -e "$WAYLAND_DISPLAY_SOCKET" ]; then
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5199230Z     WAYLAND_DISPLAY=$(cat "${WAYLAND_DISPLAY_SOCKET}")
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5199534Z     export WAYLAND_DISPLAY
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5199748Z fi
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5199850Z
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5199995Z # needed to enable a11y features on Wayland environments
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5200395Z if [ -n "$WAYLAND_DISPLAY" ] && [ -z "$AT_SPI_BUS_ADDRESS" ]; then
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5200752Z     AT_SPI_BUS_ADDRESS=$(cat "${ATSPI_BUSADDR}")
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5201046Z     export AT_SPI_BUS_ADDRESS
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5201271Z fi
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5201368Z
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5201530Z # use the correct home and config directories for system settings
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5202383Z PKEXEC_USER=$(id -un "${PKEXEC_UID}")

2026-10-25T08:44:50.5685417Z class CompositorLocaledWrapper(LocaledWrapperBase):
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5686100Z     """Localed wrapper class which is used to control compositor.
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5686535Z
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5686937Z     It adds support for layout selection and reactions on the compositor system changes.
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5687658Z     """
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5687833Z
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5687976Z     def __init__(self):
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5688345Z         super().__init__()
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5688593Z
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5688761Z         self._user_layouts_variants = []
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5689235Z         self._last_layouts_variants = []
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5689739Z
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5689953Z         self.compositor_layouts_changed = Signal()
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5690552Z         self.compositor_selected_layout_changed = Signal()
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5690960Z
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5691149Z         # to reflect updates from the compositor
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5692292Z         self._localed_proxy.PropertiesChanged.connect(self._on_properties_changed)
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5692969Z
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5693331Z     def _on_properties_changed(self, interface, changed_props, invalid_props):
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5694163Z         if "X11Layout" in changed_props or "X11Variant" in changed_props:
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5695062Z             layouts_variants = self._from_localed_format(changed_props["X11Layout"].get_string(),
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5695935Z                                                          changed_props["X11Variant"].get_string())
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5696762Z             # This part is a bit tricky. The signal processing here means that compositor has
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5697942Z             # changed current layouts configuration. This could happen for multiple reasons:
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5698726Z             # - user changed the layout in compositor
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5699293Z             # - Anaconda set the layout to compositor
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5699915Z             # - any other magic logic for compositor (we just don't know)
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5700475Z             #
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5700845Z             # The question is how we should behave:
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5701556Z             # - we don't want to take compositor layouts to Anaconda because that will change
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5702759Z             #   what user will have in the installed system.
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5703520Z             # - we don't want to force our layouts to compositor because that would forbid user
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5704353Z             #   to change compositor layout when Anaconda runs in background
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5704951Z             #
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5705569Z             # The best shot seems to just signal out that the layout has changed and nothing else.
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5705999Z
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5706173Z             # layouts has changed in compositor, always emit this signal
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5706620Z             log.debug("Localed layouts has changed. Last known: '%s' current: '%s'",
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5707059Z                       self._last_layouts_variants, layouts_variants)
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5707446Z             self.compositor_layouts_changed.emit(layouts_variants)
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5707958Z
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5708185Z             # check if last selected variant has changed
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5708746Z             # nothing is selected in compositor
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5709239Z             if not layouts_variants:
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5709742Z                 log.warning("Compositor layouts not set.")
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5710347Z                 self.compositor_selected_layout_changed.emit("")
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5710933Z             # we don't know last used layouts
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5711440Z             elif not self._last_layouts_variants:
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5712388Z                 log.debug("Compositor selected layout is different. "
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5713056Z                           "Missing information about last selected layouts.")
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5713823Z                 self.compositor_selected_layout_changed.emit(layouts_variants[0])
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5714531Z             # selected (first) has changed
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5715165Z             elif layouts_variants[0] != self._last_layouts_variants[0]:
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5715885Z                 log.debug("Compositor selected layout is different.")
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5716646Z                 self.compositor_selected_layout_changed.emit(layouts_variants[0])
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5717165Z
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5717383Z             self._last_layouts_variants = layouts_variants

2026-10-25T08:44:50.5768842Z         :rtype: bool
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5769200Z         """
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5769662Z         # ignore compositor layouts but force Anaconda configuration
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5770292Z         layouts = self._user_layouts_variants
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5770630Z
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5770759Z         try:
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5771190Z             new_layouts = self._shift_list(layouts, layout_variant)
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5772011Z             self._set_layouts(new_layouts)
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5772471Z             return True
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5772828Z         except ValueError:
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5773385Z             log.warning("Can't set layout: '%s' as first to the current set: %s",
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5774067Z                         layout_variant, layouts)
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5774562Z             return False
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5774812Z
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5774952Z     @staticmethod
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5775390Z     def _shift_list(source_layouts, value_to_first):
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5776114Z         """Helper method to reorder list of layouts and move one as first in the list.
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5776651Z
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5777033Z         We should preserve the ordering just shift items from start of the list to the
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5777736Z         end in the same order.
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5778014Z
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5778225Z         When we want to set 2nd as first in this list:
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5778766Z         ["cz", "es", "us"]
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5779193Z         The result should be:
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5779629Z         ["es", "us", "cz"]
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5780963Z
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5781238Z         So the compositor has the same next layout as Anaconda.
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5781915Z
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5782262Z         :raises: ValueError: if the list is small or the layout is not inside
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5782878Z         """
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5783302Z         value_id = source_layouts.index(value_to_first)
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5784113Z         new_list = source_layouts[value_id:len(source_layouts)] + source_layouts[0:value_id]
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5784965Z         return new_list
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5785218Z
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5785389Z     def select_next_layout(self):
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5785924Z         """Select (make it first) next layout for compositor.
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5786337Z
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5786760Z         Find current compositor layout in the list of defined layouts and set next to it as
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5787775Z         current (first) for compositor. We need to have user defined list because compositor
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5788881Z         layouts will change with the selection. Store this list when user is setting configuration
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5789837Z         to compositor. This list must not change ordering.
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5790240Z
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5790557Z         :param user_layouts: List of layouts selected by user in Anaconda.
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5791510Z         :type user_layouts: [str]
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5792506Z         :return: If switch was successful True otherwise False
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5793064Z         :rtype: bool
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5793405Z         """
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5793778Z         current_layout = self.current_layout_variant
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5794274Z         layout_id = 0
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5794501Z
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5794671Z         if not self._user_layouts_variants:
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5795397Z             log.error("Can't switch next layout - user defined keyboard layout is not present!")
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5796186Z             return False
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5796420Z
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5796563Z         # find next layout
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5797011Z         for i, v in enumerate(self._user_layouts_variants):
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5797547Z             if v == current_layout:
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5798008Z                 layout_id = i + 1
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5798514Z                 layout_id %= len(self._user_layouts_variants)
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5799136Z
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5799285Z         try:
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5799766Z             new_layouts = self._shift_list(self._user_layouts_variants,
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5800464Z                                            self._user_layouts_variants[layout_id])
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5801047Z             self._set_layouts(new_layouts)
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5801504Z             return True
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5802203Z         except ValueError:
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5802839Z             log.warning("Can't set next keyboard layout %s", self._user_layouts_variants)
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5803549Z             return False
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5803794Z

2026-10-25T08:44:50.5931496Z from pyanaconda.anaconda_loggers import get_module_logger
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5931986Z from pyanaconda.core.constants import DEFAULT_KEYBOARD

2026-10-25T08:44:50.5932480Z
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5932645Z log = get_module_logger(__name__)
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5932654Z
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5932659Z
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5933050Z def get_missing_keyboard_configuration(localed_wrapper, x_layouts, vc_keymap):
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5933273Z     """Get keyboard configuration if not set by user.
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5933280Z
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5933447Z     Algorithm works as this:
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5933687Z     1. Check if full keyboard configuration is already set
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5933829Z        -> return these
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5934111Z     2. If no X layouts, check if they can be obtained from live system
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5934251Z        -> read X layouts
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5934441Z     3a. If still no configuration is present
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5934739Z        -> use DEFAULT_KEYBOARD as console layout and fall through to...
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5935056Z     3b. If only one of the keyboard layout or virtual console keymap is set
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5935238Z        -> convert one to the other by localed
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5935244Z
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5935560Z     :param localed_wrapper: instance of systemd-localed service wrapper
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5935737Z     :type localed_wrapper: LocaledWrapper
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5935955Z     :param x_layouts: list of X layout specifications
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5936106Z     :type x_layouts: list(str)
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5936354Z     :param vc_keymap: virtual console keyboard mapping name
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5936505Z     :type vc_keymap: str
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5936729Z     :returns: tuple of X layouts and VC keyboard settings
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5936883Z     :rtype: (list(str), str))
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5937005Z     """
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5937161Z     if vc_keymap and x_layouts:
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5937575Z         log.debug("Keyboard layouts and virtual console keymap already set - nothing to do")
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5937911Z         return x_layouts, vc_keymap
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5937919Z
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5938124Z     live_keyboard = get_live_keyboard_instance()
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5938516Z     # layouts are not set by user, we should take a look for live configuration if available
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5938687Z     if not x_layouts and live_keyboard:
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5938997Z         log.debug("Keyboard configuration from Live system is available")
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5939216Z         x_layouts = live_keyboard.read_keyboard_layouts()
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5939222Z
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5939386Z     if not vc_keymap and not x_layouts:
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5939805Z         log.debug("Using default value %s for missing virtual console keymap", DEFAULT_KEYBOARD)
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5939966Z         vc_keymap = DEFAULT_KEYBOARD
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5939972Z
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5940137Z     if not vc_keymap or not x_layouts:
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5940598Z         x_layouts, vc_keymap = _resolve_missing_by_conversion(localed_wrapper, x_layouts, vc_keymap)
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5940745Z
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5940909Z     return x_layouts, vc_keymap
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5940916Z
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5940921Z
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5941269Z def _resolve_missing_by_conversion(localed_wrapper, x_layouts, vc_keymap):
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5941404Z     if not vc_keymap:
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5941655Z         vc_keymap = localed_wrapper.convert_layouts(x_layouts)
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5942555Z         log.debug("Missing virtual console keymap value %s converted from %s X layouts",
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5942727Z                   vc_keymap, x_layouts)
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5942869Z     if not x_layouts:
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5943108Z         x_layouts = localed_wrapper.convert_keymap(vc_keymap)
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5943484Z         log.debug("Missing X layouts value %s converted from %s virtual console keymap",
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5943643Z                   x_layouts, vc_keymap)
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5943649Z
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5943808Z     return x_layouts, vc_keymap

2026-10-25T08:44:50.5951114Z
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5951444Z # Run a local bridge, web server, and browser for a particular Cockpit page.
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5952022Z # This is useful for integration into .desktop files, for systems which don't
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5952541Z # have cockpit.socket enabled. The web server and browser run in an unshared
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5952910Z # network namespace, and thus are totally isolated from everything else.
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5953037Z #
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5953161Z # Usage:
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5953509Z #     cockpit-desktop /cockpit/@localhost/system/index.html -t default -r 0
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5954030Z # For testing purposes one can also make cockpit-ws bind to all addresses
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5954279Z # (this is insecure and should not be used in prodcuction):
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5954630Z #     cockpit-desktop /cockpit/@localhost/system/index.html -t default -r 1
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5954755Z #
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5954892Z set -eu
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5954900Z
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5955049Z THEME_ID="default"
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5955192Z WEBUI_REMOTE=0
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5955359Z while getopts t:r: option
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5955487Z do
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5955635Z     case "${option}"
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5955769Z         in
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5955921Z         t)THEME_ID=${OPTARG};;
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5956084Z         r)WEBUI_REMOTE=${OPTARG};;
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5956251Z         *) echo "Usage: $0 [-t THEME_ID] [-r WEBUI_REMOTE]" >&2
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5956335Z        exit 1 ;;
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5956408Z     esac
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5956483Z done
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5956496Z
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5956658Z URL_PATH="/cockpit/@localhost/anaconda-webui/index.html"
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5956662Z
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5956747Z WEBUI_ADDRESS="127.0.0.1"
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5956842Z if [[ "$WEBUI_REMOTE" == "1" ]]
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5957151Z then
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5957314Z     WEBUI_ADDRESS="0.0.0.0"
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5957441Z fi
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5957449Z
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5957748Z echo "WEBUI_ADDRESS=$WEBUI_ADDRESS" > /tmp/webui-cockpit-ws.env
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5958558Z systemctl start webui-cockpit-ws

2026-10-25T08:44:50.5958977Z setup_user_environment() {
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5959273Z     # PKEXEC_UID is the uid for "gnome-initial-setup" or "liveuser"
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5959475Z     # depending on how the installer gets started.
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5959603Z     #
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5959941Z     # It's unset on non-live-images, so we just use the current user then (root)
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5960132Z     if [ -n "${PKEXEC_UID:-}" ]; then
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5960377Z       # If PKEXEC_UID is set, use the user associated with it
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5960568Z       INSTALLER_USER=$(id -n -u "${PKEXEC_UID}")
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5960840Z     else
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5961192Z       # Try to find another user who is not root and has /usr/bin/bash as their shell
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5961653Z       INSTALLER_USER=$(grep '/usr/bin/bash' /etc/passwd | grep -v '^root' | cut -d: -f1 | head -n 1)
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5961660Z
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5962321Z       # If no such user is found, use the current user running the script
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5962492Z       if [ -z "${INSTALLER_USER:-}" ]; then
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5962655Z         INSTALLER_USER=$(id -un)
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5962786Z       fi
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5962909Z     fi
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5962916Z
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5963113Z     # Get the home directory of the selected user
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5963583Z     HOME_DIR=$(grep "^$INSTALLER_USER:" /etc/passwd | cut -d: -f6)
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5963592Z
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5963851Z     # Check if the home directory exists, otherwise create it
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5964021Z     if [ ! -d "$HOME_DIR" ]; then
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5964325Z       echo "The home directory $HOME_DIR does not exist. Creating it." >&2
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5964489Z       mkdir -p "$HOME_DIR"
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5964664Z       chown "$INSTALLER_USER" "$HOME_DIR"
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5964804Z     else
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5965051Z       echo "Home directory $HOME_DIR already exists." >&2
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5965181Z     fi
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5965189Z
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5965729Z     # Return the user and home directory for further use
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5965889Z     echo "$INSTALLER_USER"
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5966025Z }

2026-10-25T08:44:50.5966364Z get_default_browser() {
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5966813Z     # Read the default browser from the anaconda runtime configuration file but wait till it's available
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5967335Z     # FIXME: Related to: https://issues.redhat.com/browse/INSTALLER-4223
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5967466Z     timeout=60
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5967603Z     elapsed=0
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5967611Z
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5967830Z     while [ ! -f /run/anaconda/anaconda.conf ]; do
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5968005Z         if [ "$elapsed" -ge "$timeout" ]; then
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5968313Z             echo "Timed out waiting for anaconda.conf to be available." >&2
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5968453Z             exit 1
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5968601Z         fi
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5968737Z         sleep 1
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5968890Z         elapsed=$((elapsed + 1))
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5969021Z     done
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5969029Z
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5969501Z     # FIXME: Should be provided as an argument to this script instead of grepping the config file
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5969966Z     DEFAULT_BROWSER=$(grep '^webui_web_engine =' /run/anaconda/anaconda.conf | cut -d'=' -f2)
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5970173Z     if [ -n "$DEFAULT_BROWSER" ]; then
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5970499Z         echo "Default browser set in anaconda.conf: $DEFAULT_BROWSER" >&2
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5970936Z         DEFAULT_BROWSER=$(echo "$DEFAULT_BROWSER" | sed 's/^[[:space:]]*//;s/[[:space:]]*$//')
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5971118Z         echo "$DEFAULT_BROWSER"
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5971257Z     else
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5971571Z         echo "No default browser set in anaconda.conf, using firefox." >&2
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5971986Z         echo "firefox"
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5972136Z     fi
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5972267Z }

2026-10-25T08:44:50.5977613Z
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5977765Z     declare -p BROWSER
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5977887Z }

2026-10-25T08:44:50.5990314Z

2026-10-25T08:44:50.5991570Z     fi

2026-10-25T08:44:50.5992221Z     printf '\n'

2026-10-25T08:44:50.5993073Z # verbose ->> very (very!) old bootup look (prior to RHL-6.0?)
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5993249Z # color ->> default bootup look
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5993614Z # other ->> default bootup look without ANSI colors or positioning
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5993791Z BOOTUP=color
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5993958Z # Column to start "[  OK  ]" label in:
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5994103Z RES_COL=60
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5994303Z # terminal sequence to move to that column:
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5994474Z MOVE_TO_COL="printf \\033[${RES_COL}G"
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5994769Z # Terminal sequence to set color to a 'success' (bright green):
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5994945Z SETCOLOR_SUCCESS="printf \\033[1;32m"
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5995228Z # Terminal sequence to set color to a 'failure' (bright red):
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5995413Z SETCOLOR_FAILURE="printf \\033[1;31m"
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5995701Z # Terminal sequence to set color to a 'warning' (bright yellow):
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5995900Z SETCOLOR_WARNING="printf \\033[1;33m"
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5996114Z # Terminal sequence to reset to the default color:
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5996292Z SETCOLOR_NORMAL="printf \\033[0;39m"
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5996299Z
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5996470Z # NOTE: /dev/ttyS* is serial console.
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5996756Z # 'not a tty' is reported when executed under systemd service units
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5996991Z # where /dev/null is associated with standard input
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5997000Z
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5997152Z test_tty=$(LC_ALL=C tty)
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5997302Z case "$test_tty" in

2026-10-25T08:44:50.5997602Z         BOOTUP=serial
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5997746Z         MOVE_TO_COL=

2026-10-25T08:44:50.5998053Z         SETCOLOR_FAILURE=
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5998196Z         SETCOLOR_WARNING=
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5998337Z         SETCOLOR_NORMAL=
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5998475Z         ;;
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5998604Z esac

2026-10-25T08:44:50.6004506Z fscheck() {

2026-10-25T08:44:50.6004990Z     local fs="$2"
2026-10-25T08:44:50.6005130Z     local d="${3:-$2}"
2026-10-25T08:44:50.6005284Z     umount "$fs" >/dev/null 2>&1 || :
2026-10-25T08:44:50.6005426Z     case "$fstype" in
2026-10-25T08:44:50.6005551Z         ext[432])
2026-10-25T08:44:50.6005693Z             printf '\n'
2026-10-25T08:44:50.6005869Z             flock "$d" e2fsck -yfv "$fs" || :
2026-10-25T08:44:50.6005992Z             ;;
2026-10-25T08:44:50.6006127Z         vfat|msdos)
2026-10-25T08:44:50.6006262Z             printf '\n'
2026-10-25T08:44:50.6006430Z             flock "$d" fsck.fat -avVw "$fs"
2026-10-25T08:44:50.6006560Z             ;;
2026-10-25T08:44:50.6006684Z         btrfs)
2026-10-25T08:44:50.6006897Z             printf '# btrfs check -p --repair --force'
2026-10-25T08:44:50.6007133Z             flock "$d" btrfs check -p --repair --force "$fs"
2026-10-25T08:44:50.6007282Z             ;;
2026-10-25T08:44:50.6007421Z         xfs)
2026-10-25T08:44:50.6007590Z             printf '\n# xfs_repair -v'
2026-10-25T08:44:50.6007777Z             flock "$d" xfs_repair -v "$fs"
2026-10-25T08:44:50.6007910Z             ;;
2026-10-25T08:44:50.6008046Z         f2fs)
2026-10-25T08:44:50.6008214Z             printf '# fsck.f2fs -afy'
2026-10-25T08:44:50.6008400Z             flock "$d" fsck.f2fs -afy "$fs"
2026-10-25T08:44:50.6008539Z             ;;
2026-10-25T08:44:50.6008669Z         *)
2026-10-25T08:44:50.6008967Z             printf "\n\tERROR:   Filesystem checking for '%s' is not\n \
2026-10-25T08:44:50.6009130Z                 available.\n" "$fstype"
2026-10-25T08:44:50.6009268Z     esac
2026-10-25T08:44:50.6009395Z }

2026-10-25T08:44:50.6035151Z mountPersistentHome() {
2026-10-25T08:44:50.6035449Z   # support label/uuid
2026-10-25T08:44:50.6035839Z   if [ "${homedev##LABEL=}" != "$homedev" ] ||
2026-10-25T08:44:50.6036167Z     [ "${homedev##UUID=}" != "$homedev" ]; then
2026-10-25T08:44:50.6036481Z     homedev=$(blkid -o device -t "$homedev")
2026-10-25T08:44:50.6036707Z   fi
2026-10-25T08:44:50.6036842Z
2026-10-25T08:44:50.6037083Z   # if we're given a file rather than a blockdev, loop mount it
2026-10-25T08:44:50.6037431Z   if [ "${homedev##mtd}" != "$homedev" ]; then
2026-10-25T08:44:50.6038025Z     # mtd devs don't have a block device but get magic-mounted with -t jffs2
2026-10-25T08:44:50.6038315Z     mountopts="-t jffs2"
2026-10-25T08:44:50.6038584Z   elif [ ! -b "$homedev" ]; then
2026-10-25T08:44:50.6038935Z     if [ "${homedev##/run/initramfs/live}" != "$homedev" ]; then
2026-10-25T08:44:50.6039396Z       action "Remounting live store r/w" mount -o remount,rw /run/initramfs/live
2026-10-25T08:44:50.6039607Z     fi
2026-10-25T08:44:50.6039877Z     homedev=$(losetup -f --show "$homedev")
2026-10-25T08:44:50.6040090Z   fi
2026-10-25T08:44:50.6040422Z   homefs=$(blkid -s TYPE -o value "$homedev" 2>/dev/null)
2026-10-25T08:44:50.6040518Z
2026-10-25T08:44:50.6040786Z   # if it's encrypted, we need to unlock it
2026-10-25T08:44:50.6041033Z   if [ "$homefs" = "crypto_LUKS" ]; then
2026-10-25T08:44:50.6041248Z     echo
2026-10-25T08:44:50.6041519Z     echo "Setting up encrypted /home device"
2026-10-25T08:44:50.6042366Z     plymouth ask-for-password --command="cryptsetup luksOpen $homedev EncHome"
2026-10-25T08:44:50.6042655Z     homedev=/dev/mapper/EncHome
2026-10-25T08:44:50.6042874Z   fi
2026-10-25T08:44:50.6043248Z   if [ "$homedev" ] && [ "${cmdline##* rd.skipfsck[= ]}" = "$cmdline" ]; then
2026-10-25T08:44:50.6043649Z     action "Checking home filesystem" fscheck "$homefs" "$homedev"
2026-10-25T08:44:50.6043890Z   fi
2026-10-25T08:44:50.6044001Z
2026-10-25T08:44:50.6044264Z   # and finally do the mount
2026-10-25T08:44:50.6044589Z   mount ${mountopts:+$mountopts} "$homedev" /home
2026-10-25T08:44:50.6044983Z   # if we have /home under what's passed for persistent home, then
2026-10-25T08:44:50.6045374Z   # we should make that the real /home.  useful for mtd device on olpc
2026-10-25T08:44:50.6045680Z   [ -d /home/home ] && mount --bind /home/home /home
2026-10-25T08:44:50.6045973Z   [ -x /sbin/restorecon ] && /sbin/restorecon /home
2026-10-25T08:44:50.6046241Z   [ -d /home/liveuser ] && USERADDARGS="-M"
2026-10-25T08:44:50.6046472Z }
2026-10-25T08:44:50.6046569Z
2026-10-25T08:44:50.6046830Z cut=${cmdline##* persistenthome[= ]}
2026-10-25T08:44:50.6047092Z if [ ${#cut} -ne ${#cmdline} ]; then
2026-10-25T08:44:50.6047322Z   homedev=${cut%% *}
2026-10-25T08:44:50.6047660Z elif [ -e /run/initramfs/live/"${livedir}"/home.img ]; then

2026-10-25T08:44:50.5457339Z

2026-10-25T08:44:50.5686096Z     """Localed wrapper class which is used to control compositor.
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5686532Z
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5686933Z     It adds support for layout selection and reactions on the compositor system changes.
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5687654Z     """
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5687829Z
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5687973Z     def __init__(self):
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5688341Z         super().__init__()
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5688590Z
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5688752Z         self._user_layouts_variants = []

2026-10-25T08:44:50.5689552Z
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5689949Z         self.compositor_layouts_changed = Signal()
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5690549Z         self.compositor_selected_layout_changed = Signal()
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5690957Z
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5691145Z         # to reflect updates from the compositor
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5692284Z         self._localed_proxy.PropertiesChanged.connect(self._on_properties_changed)
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5692964Z
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5693328Z     def _on_properties_changed(self, interface, changed_props, invalid_props):
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5694159Z         if "X11Layout" in changed_props or "X11Variant" in changed_props:
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5695057Z             layouts_variants = self._from_localed_format(changed_props["X11Layout"].get_string(),
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5695932Z                                                          changed_props["X11Variant"].get_string())
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5696758Z             # This part is a bit tricky. The signal processing here means that compositor has
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5697931Z             # changed current layouts configuration. This could happen for multiple reasons:
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5698721Z             # - user changed the layout in compositor
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5699288Z             # - Anaconda set the layout to compositor
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5699912Z             # - any other magic logic for compositor (we just don't know)
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5700471Z             #
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5700836Z             # The question is how we should behave:
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5701552Z             # - we don't want to take compositor layouts to Anaconda because that will change
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5702753Z             #   what user will have in the installed system.
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5703516Z             # - we don't want to force our layouts to compositor because that would forbid user
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5704349Z             #   to change compositor layout when Anaconda runs in background
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5704945Z             #
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5705556Z             # The best shot seems to just signal out that the layout has changed and nothing else.
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5705994Z
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5706171Z             # layouts has changed in compositor, always emit this signal
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5706617Z             log.debug("Localed layouts has changed. Last known: '%s' current: '%s'",
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5707057Z                       self._last_layouts_variants, layouts_variants)
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5707444Z             self.compositor_layouts_changed.emit(layouts_variants)
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5707952Z
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5708179Z             # check if last selected variant has changed
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5708742Z             # nothing is selected in compositor
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5709235Z             if not layouts_variants:
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5709738Z                 log.warning("Compositor layouts not set.")
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5710344Z                 self.compositor_selected_layout_changed.emit("")
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5710923Z             # we don't know last used layouts
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5711436Z             elif not self._last_layouts_variants:
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5712382Z                 log.debug("Compositor selected layout is different. "
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5713052Z                           "Missing information about last selected layouts.")
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5713818Z                 self.compositor_selected_layout_changed.emit(layouts_variants[0])
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5714524Z             # selected (first) has changed
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5715153Z             elif layouts_variants[0] != self._last_layouts_variants[0]:
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5715881Z                 log.debug("Compositor selected layout is different.")
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5716643Z                 self.compositor_selected_layout_changed.emit(layouts_variants[0])
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5717154Z
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5717380Z             self._last_layouts_variants = layouts_variants

2026-10-25T08:44:50.5736696Z         log.debug("Storing layouts for compositor configured by user")
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5737350Z         self._user_layouts_variants = layouts_variants
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5737715Z
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5738051Z     def _set_layouts(self, layouts_variants, options=None, convert=False):
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5738769Z         """Set a new layouts for compositor.
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5739121Z
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5739415Z         Extension of the parent code with compositor specific code.
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5740011Z         """
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5740597Z         # If options are not set let's use from a system so we don't change the system settings
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5741325Z         if options is None:
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5741956Z             options = self.options
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5742405Z             log.debug(
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5743091Z                 "Keyboard layouts for compositor are missing options. Use compositor options: %s",
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5743858Z                 options,
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5744221Z             )
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5744403Z
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5744819Z         # Disable keyboard layout switching by keyboard shortcut as it is hard to make this
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5745811Z         # working from the system to localed. The current layout is in general problematic to
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5746513Z         # decide.
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5746838Z         log.debug(
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5747463Z             "Disable keyboard layouts switching shortcut to compositor from the options: '%s'",
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5748215Z             options,
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5748560Z         )
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5749041Z         options = list(filter(lambda x: not x.startswith("grp:"), options))
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5749743Z
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5749912Z         # store configuration from user
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5750468Z         super().set_layouts(layouts_variants, options, convert)
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5750888Z
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5751075Z     def select_layout(self, layout_variant):
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5751640Z         """Select layout from the list of current layouts set.
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5752438Z
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5752814Z         This will search for the given layout variant in the list and move it as first
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5753650Z         in the list. The first layout in systemd is taken as the used one.
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5754124Z
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5754464Z         :param layout_variant: The layout to set, with format "layout (variant)"
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5755127Z             (e.g. "cz (qwerty)")
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5767511Z         :type layout_variant: str
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5768306Z         :return: If the keyboard layout was activated
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5768837Z         :rtype: bool
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5769196Z         """
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5769658Z         # ignore compositor layouts but force Anaconda configuration
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5770288Z         layouts = self._user_layouts_variants
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5770625Z
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5770756Z         try:
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5771187Z             new_layouts = self._shift_list(layouts, layout_variant)
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5772006Z             self._set_layouts(new_layouts)
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5772468Z             return True
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5772824Z         except ValueError:
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5773381Z             log.warning("Can't set layout: '%s' as first to the current set: %s",
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5774060Z                         layout_variant, layouts)
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5774558Z             return False
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5774807Z
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5774949Z     @staticmethod
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5775380Z     def _shift_list(source_layouts, value_to_first):
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5776110Z         """Helper method to reorder list of layouts and move one as first in the list.
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5776648Z
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5777029Z         We should preserve the ordering just shift items from start of the list to the
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5777732Z         end in the same order.
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5778010Z
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5778221Z         When we want to set 2nd as first in this list:
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5778759Z         ["cz", "es", "us"]
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5779187Z         The result should be:
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5779623Z         ["es", "us", "cz"]
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5780952Z
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5781233Z         So the compositor has the same next layout as Anaconda.
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5781907Z
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5782257Z         :raises: ValueError: if the list is small or the layout is not inside
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5782865Z         """
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5783296Z         value_id = source_layouts.index(value_to_first)
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5784101Z         new_list = source_layouts[value_id:len(source_layouts)] + source_layouts[0:value_id]
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5784960Z         return new_list
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5785213Z
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5785384Z     def select_next_layout(self):
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5785918Z         """Select (make it first) next layout for compositor.
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5786332Z
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5786755Z         Find current compositor layout in the list of defined layouts and set next to it as
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5787769Z         current (first) for compositor. We need to have user defined list because compositor
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5788859Z         layouts will change with the selection. Store this list when user is setting configuration
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5789831Z         to compositor. This list must not change ordering.
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5790235Z
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5790552Z         :param user_layouts: List of layouts selected by user in Anaconda.
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5791308Z         :type user_layouts: [str]
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5792497Z         :return: If switch was successful True otherwise False
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5793059Z         :rtype: bool
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5793401Z         """
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5793774Z         current_layout = self.current_layout_variant
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5794271Z         layout_id = 0
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5794497Z
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5794667Z         if not self._user_layouts_variants:
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5795393Z             log.error("Can't switch next layout - user defined keyboard layout is not present!")
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5796181Z             return False
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5796416Z
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5796559Z         # find next layout
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5797008Z         for i, v in enumerate(self._user_layouts_variants):
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5797542Z             if v == current_layout:
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5798001Z                 layout_id = i + 1
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5798508Z                 layout_id %= len(self._user_layouts_variants)
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5799119Z
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5799281Z         try:
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5799761Z             new_layouts = self._shift_list(self._user_layouts_variants,
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5800459Z                                            self._user_layouts_variants[layout_id])
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5801043Z             self._set_layouts(new_layouts)
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5801500Z             return True
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5802197Z         except ValueError:
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5802832Z             log.warning("Can't set next keyboard layout %s", self._user_layouts_variants)
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5803544Z             return False

2026-10-25T08:44:50.5931492Z from pyanaconda.anaconda_loggers import get_module_logger
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5931981Z from pyanaconda.core.constants import DEFAULT_KEYBOARD

2026-10-25T08:44:50.5932475Z
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5932640Z log = get_module_logger(__name__)
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5932651Z
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5932657Z
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5933045Z def get_missing_keyboard_configuration(localed_wrapper, x_layouts, vc_keymap):
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5933269Z     """Get keyboard configuration if not set by user.
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5933278Z
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5933442Z     Algorithm works as this:
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5933684Z     1. Check if full keyboard configuration is already set
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5933826Z        -> return these
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5934107Z     2. If no X layouts, check if they can be obtained from live system
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5934248Z        -> read X layouts
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5934437Z     3a. If still no configuration is present
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5934736Z        -> use DEFAULT_KEYBOARD as console layout and fall through to...
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5935048Z     3b. If only one of the keyboard layout or virtual console keymap is set
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5935235Z        -> convert one to the other by localed
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5935241Z
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5935552Z     :param localed_wrapper: instance of systemd-localed service wrapper
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5935733Z     :type localed_wrapper: LocaledWrapper
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5935952Z     :param x_layouts: list of X layout specifications
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5936103Z     :type x_layouts: list(str)
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5936351Z     :param vc_keymap: virtual console keyboard mapping name
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5936496Z     :type vc_keymap: str
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5936726Z     :returns: tuple of X layouts and VC keyboard settings
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5936880Z     :rtype: (list(str), str))
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5937002Z     """
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5937157Z     if vc_keymap and x_layouts:
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5937571Z         log.debug("Keyboard layouts and virtual console keymap already set - nothing to do")
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5937739Z         return x_layouts, vc_keymap
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5937916Z
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5938119Z     live_keyboard = get_live_keyboard_instance()
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5938512Z     # layouts are not set by user, we should take a look for live configuration if available
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5938683Z     if not x_layouts and live_keyboard:
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5938994Z         log.debug("Keyboard configuration from Live system is available")
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5939213Z         x_layouts = live_keyboard.read_keyboard_layouts()
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5939220Z
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5939383Z     if not vc_keymap and not x_layouts:
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5939801Z         log.debug("Using default value %s for missing virtual console keymap", DEFAULT_KEYBOARD)
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5939962Z         vc_keymap = DEFAULT_KEYBOARD
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5939969Z
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5940133Z     if not vc_keymap or not x_layouts:
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5940587Z         x_layouts, vc_keymap = _resolve_missing_by_conversion(localed_wrapper, x_layouts, vc_keymap)
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5940742Z
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5940906Z     return x_layouts, vc_keymap
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5940914Z
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5940919Z
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5941265Z def _resolve_missing_by_conversion(localed_wrapper, x_layouts, vc_keymap):
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5941401Z     if not vc_keymap:
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5941652Z         vc_keymap = localed_wrapper.convert_layouts(x_layouts)
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5942548Z         log.debug("Missing virtual console keymap value %s converted from %s X layouts",
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5942724Z                   vc_keymap, x_layouts)
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5942866Z     if not x_layouts:
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5943105Z         x_layouts = localed_wrapper.convert_keymap(vc_keymap)
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5943481Z         log.debug("Missing X layouts value %s converted from %s virtual console keymap",
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5943639Z                   x_layouts, vc_keymap)
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5943646Z
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5943805Z     return x_layouts, vc_keymap
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5943822Z

2026-10-25T08:44:50.5951441Z # Run a local bridge, web server, and browser for a particular Cockpit page.
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5952017Z # This is useful for integration into .desktop files, for systems which don't
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5952535Z # have cockpit.socket enabled. The web server and browser run in an unshared
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5952906Z # network namespace, and thus are totally isolated from everything else.
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5953034Z #
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5953158Z # Usage:
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5953506Z #     cockpit-desktop /cockpit/@localhost/system/index.html -t default -r 0
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5954018Z # For testing purposes one can also make cockpit-ws bind to all addresses
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5954274Z # (this is insecure and should not be used in prodcuction):
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5954626Z #     cockpit-desktop /cockpit/@localhost/system/index.html -t default -r 1
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5954751Z #
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5954888Z set -eu
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5954897Z
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5955045Z THEME_ID="default"
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5955187Z WEBUI_REMOTE=0
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5955355Z while getopts t:r: option
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5955483Z do
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5955631Z     case "${option}"
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5955760Z         in
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5955917Z         t)THEME_ID=${OPTARG};;
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5956080Z         r)WEBUI_REMOTE=${OPTARG};;
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5956248Z         *) echo "Usage: $0 [-t THEME_ID] [-r WEBUI_REMOTE]" >&2
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5956332Z        exit 1 ;;
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5956406Z     esac
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5956481Z done
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5956494Z
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5956651Z URL_PATH="/cockpit/@localhost/anaconda-webui/index.html"
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5956661Z
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5956745Z WEBUI_ADDRESS="127.0.0.1"
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5956840Z if [[ "$WEBUI_REMOTE" == "1" ]]
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5957146Z then
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5957310Z     WEBUI_ADDRESS="0.0.0.0"
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5957437Z fi
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5957446Z
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5957743Z echo "WEBUI_ADDRESS=$WEBUI_ADDRESS" > /tmp/webui-cockpit-ws.env
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5958539Z systemctl start webui-cockpit-ws
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5958570Z
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5958803Z # Function to setup user-related environment
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5958974Z setup_user_environment() {
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5959269Z     # PKEXEC_UID is the uid for "gnome-initial-setup" or "liveuser"
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5959472Z     # depending on how the installer gets started.
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5959600Z     #
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5959938Z     # It's unset on non-live-images, so we just use the current user then (root)
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5960121Z     if [ -n "${PKEXEC_UID:-}" ]; then
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5960374Z       # If PKEXEC_UID is set, use the user associated with it
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5960564Z       INSTALLER_USER=$(id -n -u "${PKEXEC_UID}")
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5960836Z     else
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5961188Z       # Try to find another user who is not root and has /usr/bin/bash as their shell
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5961649Z       INSTALLER_USER=$(grep '/usr/bin/bash' /etc/passwd | grep -v '^root' | cut -d: -f1 | head -n 1)
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5961657Z
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5962316Z       # If no such user is found, use the current user running the script
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5962488Z       if [ -z "${INSTALLER_USER:-}" ]; then
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5962652Z         INSTALLER_USER=$(id -un)
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5962783Z       fi
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5962906Z     fi
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5962913Z
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5963110Z     # Get the home directory of the selected user
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5963381Z     HOME_DIR=$(grep "^$INSTALLER_USER:" /etc/passwd | cut -d: -f6)
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5963590Z
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5963847Z     # Check if the home directory exists, otherwise create it
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5964018Z     if [ ! -d "$HOME_DIR" ]; then
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5964320Z       echo "The home directory $HOME_DIR does not exist. Creating it." >&2
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5964485Z       mkdir -p "$HOME_DIR"
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5964660Z       chown "$INSTALLER_USER" "$HOME_DIR"
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5964800Z     else
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5965046Z       echo "Home directory $HOME_DIR already exists." >&2
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5965177Z     fi
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5965186Z
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5965723Z     # Return the user and home directory for further use
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5965885Z     echo "$INSTALLER_USER"
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5966021Z }
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5966029Z
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5966360Z get_default_browser() {
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5966808Z     # Read the default browser from the anaconda runtime configuration file but wait till it's available
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5967323Z     # FIXME: Related to: https://issues.redhat.com/browse/INSTALLER-4223
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5967462Z     timeout=60
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5967599Z     elapsed=0
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5967608Z
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5967826Z     while [ ! -f /run/anaconda/anaconda.conf ]; do
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5968000Z         if [ "$elapsed" -ge "$timeout" ]; then
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5968308Z             echo "Timed out waiting for anaconda.conf to be available." >&2
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5968447Z             exit 1
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5968597Z         fi
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5968732Z         sleep 1
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5968886Z         elapsed=$((elapsed + 1))
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5969018Z     done
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5969026Z
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5969494Z     # FIXME: Should be provided as an argument to this script instead of grepping the config file
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5969961Z     DEFAULT_BROWSER=$(grep '^webui_web_engine =' /run/anaconda/anaconda.conf | cut -d'=' -f2)
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5970161Z     if [ -n "$DEFAULT_BROWSER" ]; then
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5970492Z         echo "Default browser set in anaconda.conf: $DEFAULT_BROWSER" >&2
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5970928Z         DEFAULT_BROWSER=$(echo "$DEFAULT_BROWSER" | sed 's/^[[:space:]]*//;s/[[:space:]]*$//')
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5971103Z         echo "$DEFAULT_BROWSER"
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5971252Z     else
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5971567Z         echo "No default browser set in anaconda.conf, using firefox." >&2
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5971978Z         echo "firefox"
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5972132Z     fi
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5972263Z }
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5972272Z
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5972434Z prepare_firefox_cmd() {
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5972580Z     INSTALLER_USER=$1
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5972587Z
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5972960Z     # prepare empty firefox profile dir with theme based on the passed profile id
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5973211Z     FIREFOX_THEME_DIR="/usr/share/anaconda/firefox-theme"
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5973532Z     FIREFOX_PROFILE_PATH="${XDG_RUNTIME_DIR}/anaconda/firefox-profile"
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5973559Z
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5973826Z     # make sure the profile directory exists and is empty
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5974010Z     if [ -d ${FIREFOX_PROFILE_PATH} ]
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5974139Z     then
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5974464Z         echo "Cleaning up existing Anaconda Firefox profile directory." >&2
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5974635Z         rm -rf ${FIREFOX_PROFILE_PATH}
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5974761Z     fi
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5975062Z     pkexec --user "${INSTALLER_USER}" mkdir -p ${FIREFOX_PROFILE_PATH}
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5975070Z
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5975354Z     # populate the profile directory with our custom Firefox theme
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5975628Z     # - theme id is passed as the second argument of this script
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5975833Z     THEME_PATH="${FIREFOX_THEME_DIR}/${THEME_ID}"
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5975842Z
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5976182Z     pkexec --user "${INSTALLER_USER}" cp -a "${THEME_PATH}/." ${FIREFOX_PROFILE_PATH}
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5976203Z
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5976583Z     # set environment for Firefox
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5977057Z     BROWSER_ENV=(MOZ_APP_TITLE="" MOZ_APP_REMOTINGNAME="liveinst" MOZ_GTK_TITLEBAR_DECORATION=client)
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5977075Z
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5977600Z     BROWSER=(/usr/bin/env "${BROWSER_ENV[@]}" /usr/bin/firefox --new-instance --profile "${FIREFOX_PROFILE_PATH}")
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5977610Z
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5977761Z     declare -p BROWSER
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5977884Z }
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5977892Z
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5977902Z
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5978048Z if [[ "$WEBUI_REMOTE" == "1" ]]
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5978176Z then
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5978329Z     /bin/sleep infinity &
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5978477Z     BLOCK_ON_PID=$!
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5978603Z else
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5978779Z     USER_INFO=$(setup_user_environment)
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5979019Z     INSTALLER_USER=$(echo "$USER_INFO" | cut -d' ' -f1)
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5979211Z     HOME_DIR=$(echo "$USER_INFO" | cut -d' ' -f2)
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5979411Z
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5979666Z     # if we have netcat, use it for waiting until ws is up
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5979827Z     if type nc >/dev/null 2>&1; then
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5979978Z         for _ in `seq 10`; do
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5980161Z             nc -z "$WEBUI_ADDRESS" 80 && break
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5980301Z             sleep 0.5;
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5980436Z         done
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5980563Z     else
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5980732Z         # otherwise, just wait a bit
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5980868Z         sleep 3
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5980987Z     fi
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5980994Z
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5982215Z     readarray -t user_environment < <(pkexec --user "${INSTALLER_USER}" env XDG_RUNTIME_DIR="${XDG_RUNTIME_DIR}" systemctl --user show-environment)
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5982232Z
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5982437Z     DEFAULT_BROWSER_CMD=$(get_default_browser)
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5982702Z     echo "Using default browser: ${DEFAULT_BROWSER_CMD}" >&2
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5982925Z     if [[ "${DEFAULT_BROWSER_CMD}" == "firefox" ]]; then
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5983133Z         eval "$(prepare_firefox_cmd "$INSTALLER_USER")"
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5983388Z         echo "Using Firefox browser command: ${BROWSER[@]}" >&2
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5983510Z     else
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5983669Z         BROWSER=(${DEFAULT_BROWSER_CMD})
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5983903Z         echo "Using default browser command: ${BROWSER[@]}" >&2
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5984022Z     fi
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5984029Z
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5984474Z     # start browser in a temporary home dir, so that it does not interfere with your real one
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5984818Z     BROWSER_HOME=$(mktemp --directory --tmpdir cockpit.desktop.XXXXXX)
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5985039Z     HOME="$BROWSER_HOME" XDG_CURRENT_DESKTOP=GNOME \
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5985721Z     pkexec --user $INSTALLER_USER env DISPLAY=$DISPLAY "${user_environment[@]}" "${BROWSER[@]}" http://"$WEBUI_ADDRESS""$URL_PATH" &
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5985895Z     BLOCK_ON_PID=$!
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5986041Z fi

2026-10-25T08:44:50.5988110Z # Check if gettext exists

2026-10-25T08:44:50.5988537Z   # If not, create a dummy function that returns the input verbatim

2026-10-25T08:44:50.5990004Z # Run some action. Log its output.
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5990153Z action() {
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5990302Z     local STRING rc
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5990311Z
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5990457Z     STRING=$1
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5990639Z     printf '%s' "$(gettext "${STRING} ")"
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5990785Z     shift
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5990933Z     if "$@"; then
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5991109Z         success "$(gettext "${STRING} ")"
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5991255Z     else
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5991426Z         failure "$(gettext "${STRING} ")"
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5991566Z     fi
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5992056Z     rc=$?
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5992215Z     printf '\n'
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5992626Z     return $rc
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5992756Z }
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5992778Z
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5993067Z # verbose ->> very (very!) old bootup look (prior to RHL-6.0?)
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5993245Z # color ->> default bootup look
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5993611Z # other ->> default bootup look without ANSI colors or positioning
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5993788Z BOOTUP=color
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5993953Z # Column to start "[  OK  ]" label in:
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5994099Z RES_COL=60
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5994297Z # terminal sequence to move to that column:
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5994470Z MOVE_TO_COL="printf \\033[${RES_COL}G"
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5994764Z # Terminal sequence to set color to a 'success' (bright green):
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5994941Z SETCOLOR_SUCCESS="printf \\033[1;32m"
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5995223Z # Terminal sequence to set color to a 'failure' (bright red):
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5995409Z SETCOLOR_FAILURE="printf \\033[1;31m"
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5995696Z # Terminal sequence to set color to a 'warning' (bright yellow):
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5995889Z SETCOLOR_WARNING="printf \\033[1;33m"
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5996109Z # Terminal sequence to reset to the default color:
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5996288Z SETCOLOR_NORMAL="printf \\033[0;39m"
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5996296Z
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5996466Z # NOTE: /dev/ttyS* is serial console.
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5996752Z # 'not a tty' is reported when executed under systemd service units
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5996986Z # where /dev/null is associated with standard input
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5996997Z
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5997148Z test_tty=$(LC_ALL=C tty)
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5997299Z case "$test_tty" in
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5997456Z     /dev/ttyS* | 'not a tty')
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5997599Z         BOOTUP=serial
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5997743Z         MOVE_TO_COL=
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5997898Z         SETCOLOR_SUCCESS=
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5998049Z         SETCOLOR_FAILURE=
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5998193Z         SETCOLOR_WARNING=
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5998334Z         SETCOLOR_NORMAL=
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5998472Z         ;;
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5998595Z esac
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5998609Z
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5998773Z # Log that something succeeded
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5998911Z success() {
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5999089Z     [ "$BOOTUP" != "verbose" ] && echo_success
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5999226Z     return 0
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5999348Z }
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5999355Z
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5999508Z # Log that something failed
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5999640Z failure() {
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5999779Z     local rc=$?
2026-10-25T08:44:50.5999960Z     [ "$BOOTUP" != "verbose" ] && echo_failure
2026-10-25T08:44:50.6000175Z     [ -x /bin/plymouth ] && /bin/plymouth --details
2026-10-25T08:44:50.6000317Z     return $rc
2026-10-25T08:44:50.6000444Z }
2026-10-25T08:44:50.6000453Z
2026-10-25T08:44:50.6000601Z echo_success() {
2026-10-25T08:44:50.6000770Z     [ "$BOOTUP" = "color" ] && $MOVE_TO_COL
2026-10-25T08:44:50.6000908Z     printf '%s' "["
2026-10-25T08:44:50.6001090Z     [ "$BOOTUP" = "color" ] && $SETCOLOR_SUCCESS
2026-10-25T08:44:50.6001256Z     printf '%s' "$(gettext "  OK  ")"
2026-10-25T08:44:50.6001578Z     [ "$BOOTUP" = "color" ] && $SETCOLOR_NORMAL
2026-10-25T08:44:50.6002387Z     printf '%s' "]"
2026-10-25T08:44:50.6002561Z     printf '\r'
2026-10-25T08:44:50.6002710Z     return 0
2026-10-25T08:44:50.6002839Z }
2026-10-25T08:44:50.6002849Z
2026-10-25T08:44:50.6002994Z echo_failure() {
2026-10-25T08:44:50.6003167Z     [ "$BOOTUP" = "color" ] && $MOVE_TO_COL
2026-10-25T08:44:50.6003309Z     printf '%s' "["
2026-10-25T08:44:50.6003496Z     [ "$BOOTUP" = "color" ] && $SETCOLOR_FAILURE
2026-10-25T08:44:50.6003658Z     printf '%s' "$(gettext "FAILED")"
2026-10-25T08:44:50.6003840Z     [ "$BOOTUP" = "color" ] && $SETCOLOR_NORMAL
2026-10-25T08:44:50.6003980Z     printf '%s' "]"
2026-10-25T08:44:50.6004111Z     printf '\r'
2026-10-25T08:44:50.6004240Z     return 1
2026-10-25T08:44:50.6004363Z }

2026-10-25T08:44:50.6004503Z fscheck() {
2026-10-25T08:44:50.6004643Z     local fstype="$1"
2026-10-25T08:44:50.6004980Z     local fs="$2"
2026-10-25T08:44:50.6005126Z     local d="${3:-$2}"
2026-10-25T08:44:50.6005281Z     umount "$fs" >/dev/null 2>&1 || :
2026-10-25T08:44:50.6005423Z     case "$fstype" in
2026-10-25T08:44:50.6005548Z         ext[432])
2026-10-25T08:44:50.6005691Z             printf '\n'
2026-10-25T08:44:50.6005865Z             flock "$d" e2fsck -yfv "$fs" || :
2026-10-25T08:44:50.6005989Z             ;;
2026-10-25T08:44:50.6006124Z         vfat|msdos)
2026-10-25T08:44:50.6006259Z             printf '\n'
2026-10-25T08:44:50.6006427Z             flock "$d" fsck.fat -avVw "$fs"
2026-10-25T08:44:50.6006557Z             ;;
2026-10-25T08:44:50.6006681Z         btrfs)
2026-10-25T08:44:50.6006893Z             printf '# btrfs check -p --repair --force'
2026-10-25T08:44:50.6007129Z             flock "$d" btrfs check -p --repair --force "$fs"
2026-10-25T08:44:50.6007278Z             ;;
2026-10-25T08:44:50.6007417Z         xfs)
2026-10-25T08:44:50.6007586Z             printf '\n# xfs_repair -v'
2026-10-25T08:44:50.6007768Z             flock "$d" xfs_repair -v "$fs"
2026-10-25T08:44:50.6007907Z             ;;
2026-10-25T08:44:50.6008043Z         f2fs)
2026-10-25T08:44:50.6008210Z             printf '# fsck.f2fs -afy'
2026-10-25T08:44:50.6008396Z             flock "$d" fsck.f2fs -afy "$fs"
2026-10-25T08:44:50.6008535Z             ;;
2026-10-25T08:44:50.6008666Z         *)
2026-10-25T08:44:50.6008963Z             printf "\n\tERROR:   Filesystem checking for '%s' is not\n \
2026-10-25T08:44:50.6009126Z                 available.\n" "$fstype"
2026-10-25T08:44:50.6009265Z     esac
2026-10-25T08:44:50.6009392Z }

2026-10-25T08:44:50.6035147Z mountPersistentHome() {
2026-10-25T08:44:50.6035443Z   # support label/uuid
2026-10-25T08:44:50.6035834Z   if [ "${homedev##LABEL=}" != "$homedev" ] ||
2026-10-25T08:44:50.6036162Z     [ "${homedev##UUID=}" != "$homedev" ]; then
2026-10-25T08:44:50.6036476Z     homedev=$(blkid -o device -t "$homedev")
2026-10-25T08:44:50.6036704Z   fi
2026-10-25T08:44:50.6036839Z
2026-10-25T08:44:50.6037080Z   # if we're given a file rather than a blockdev, loop mount it
2026-10-25T08:44:50.6037423Z   if [ "${homedev##mtd}" != "$homedev" ]; then
2026-10-25T08:44:50.6038017Z     # mtd devs don't have a block device but get magic-mounted with -t jffs2
2026-10-25T08:44:50.6038310Z     mountopts="-t jffs2"
2026-10-25T08:44:50.6038580Z   elif [ ! -b "$homedev" ]; then
2026-10-25T08:44:50.6038931Z     if [ "${homedev##/run/initramfs/live}" != "$homedev" ]; then
2026-10-25T08:44:50.6039392Z       action "Remounting live store r/w" mount -o remount,rw /run/initramfs/live
2026-10-25T08:44:50.6039604Z     fi
2026-10-25T08:44:50.6039874Z     homedev=$(losetup -f --show "$homedev")
2026-10-25T08:44:50.6040087Z   fi
2026-10-25T08:44:50.6040419Z   homefs=$(blkid -s TYPE -o value "$homedev" 2>/dev/null)
2026-10-25T08:44:50.6040514Z
2026-10-25T08:44:50.6040782Z   # if it's encrypted, we need to unlock it
2026-10-25T08:44:50.6041029Z   if [ "$homefs" = "crypto_LUKS" ]; then
2026-10-25T08:44:50.6041245Z     echo
2026-10-25T08:44:50.6041515Z     echo "Setting up encrypted /home device"
2026-10-25T08:44:50.6042352Z     plymouth ask-for-password --command="cryptsetup luksOpen $homedev EncHome"
2026-10-25T08:44:50.6042650Z     homedev=/dev/mapper/EncHome
2026-10-25T08:44:50.6042870Z   fi
2026-10-25T08:44:50.6043243Z   if [ "$homedev" ] && [ "${cmdline##* rd.skipfsck[= ]}" = "$cmdline" ]; then
2026-10-25T08:44:50.6043645Z     action "Checking home filesystem" fscheck "$homefs" "$homedev"
2026-10-25T08:44:50.6043885Z   fi
2026-10-25T08:44:50.6043996Z
2026-10-25T08:44:50.6044259Z   # and finally do the mount
2026-10-25T08:44:50.6044584Z   mount ${mountopts:+$mountopts} "$homedev" /home
2026-10-25T08:44:50.6044972Z   # if we have /home under what's passed for persistent home, then
2026-10-25T08:44:50.6045370Z   # we should make that the real /home.  useful for mtd device on olpc
2026-10-25T08:44:50.6045677Z   [ -d /home/home ] && mount --bind /home/home /home
2026-10-25T08:44:50.6045970Z   [ -x /sbin/restorecon ] && /sbin/restorecon /home
2026-10-25T08:44:50.6046237Z   [ -d /home/liveuser ] && USERADDARGS="-M"
2026-10-25T08:44:50.6046463Z }

2026-10-25T08:44:50.6047318Z   homedev=${cut%% *}
